OpenWhispr/openwhispr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.11.01.11.0 Stable2026-10-29T11:43:27Z

OpenWhispr 1.11.0

Teams can now share a dictionary, the assistant can follow skills you keep for recurring work, and a bunch of new models.

🌟 Highlights

  • 📚 Shared team dictionary — Business and Enterprise members get their team's words in dictation and meeting notes. Your own words still come first.
  • 🧩 Skills — Save instructions for recurring work in Integrations → Skills. The assistant picks a fitting skill on its own.
  • 🤖 New models — GPT-6.1 Sol, Gemini 3.8 Flash, Claude 5.5 on Bedrock, Gemma 4, Qwen3.8 and more. Older models keep working if you already use them.
  • 🏢 Workspace switcher — Switch workspaces and invite people from the sidebar. Your account now lives under your avatar in the top bar.
  • ⚡ Whisper GPU fallback — Whisper checks that CUDA or Vulkan actually started and falls back to CPU if not.

🛠 Fixes

  • The app now starts after a second account signs in on the same device.
  • On macOS, slow apps get your transcript, not your old clipboard. On Windows, AltGr no longer triggers Ctrl+Alt hotkeys.
  • Meeting transcripts survive recording again, uploaded audio lands in the Transcript tab, and meeting prompts show up when they should.
  • Dictionary sync no longer drops words on a new device, and moved folders keep their chats.
  • Linux: paste works in Omarchy agent terminals, and Hyprland configs are written safely.

Full details in the changelog.

🙏 Thanks

Thanks to @greatcoat, @boseq, @Chadpiha, @IdrisGit, @hsusul, @widechaos, @etzelm, @hsearcy, @devanshc777 and @Adityakk9031, and to @tadsn3w, @hello-sebastian, @timh-86, @0xShinRai, @Hannes1909 and @graysky2 for their bug reports. 🎉

opendatalab/MinerU ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
mineru-4.1.0-releasedMinerU 4.1.0 Stable2026-10-29T11:09:59Z

中文

  • 主流平台与 GPU 自动加速：默认安装支持 Windows、Linux 和 macOS 上的 CPU 运行，以及 Intel、AMD、NVIDIA 和 Apple Silicon 兼容独显或集显的推理加速。自动选择合适的 GPU 或 CPU；代表性核心推理场景的速度约为 CPU 的 2～3 倍。
  • 统一 Tasks 异步接口：以 /v1/tasks 替换旧 /v1/uploads、/v1/parse/jobs 工作流，支持文件清单、URL 输入、缓存免传、逐文件执行与结果下载，以及统一的状态查询和取消操作。详见 Tasks 接口说明。
  • 可恢复的同步解析服务：独立的 file-parse-server 提供 /v1/file_parse，支持单文件上传和附件返回、等待超时后查询，以及通过请求 ID 恢复已知任务。详见 file-parse 接口说明。
  • 硬件诊断与档位推荐：新增 mineru-kit hardware detect 和 hardware info，帮助选择合适的服务档位，并查看设备、运行环境与校准信息。

升级提示： Python 支持范围调整为 3.11–3.14。此次包含破坏性 HTTP 接口变更，请协调升级服务端、Router、SDK 和客户端，详见 4.1 迁移说明。

English

  • Automatic acceleration across major platforms and GPUs: The default installation supports CPU inference on Windows, Linux, and macOS, plus accelerated inference on compatible Intel, AMD, NVIDIA, and Apple Silicon GPUs, including integrated graphics. MinerU automatically selects suitable GPU or CPU execution; representative core inference workloads run at approximately 2–3× CPU speed.
  • A unified asynchronous Tasks API: /v1/tasks replaces the previous /v1/uploads and /v1/parse/jobs workflow, supporting file manifests, URL inputs, cached-upload reuse, per-file execution and downloads, status queries, and cancellation. See the Tasks API reference.
  • Recoverable synchronous file parsing: The standalone file-parse-server provides /v1/file_parse for single-file uploads and attachment responses, with queries after wait timeouts and request IDs for recovering known tasks. See the file-parse API reference.
  • Hardware diagnostics and tier recommendations: New mineru-kit hardware detect and hardware info commands help select a suitable service tier and inspect devices, runtime versions, and calibration information.

Upgrade note: Supported Python versions are now 3.11–3.14. This release includes breaking HTTP API changes; upgrade servers, Routers, SDKs, and clients together. See the 4.1 migration guide.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/compare/mineru-4.0.12-released...mineru-4.1.0-released

v0.8.216 Stable2026-10-29T10:25:01Z

HyperFrames v0.8.216

Released on 2026-10-29.

This release carries every change from v0.8.215, which never reached npm. iPhone Safari keeps every take's sound, multi-worker renders land each frame in place, and encoding runs faster.

Features

  • Telemetry: Identify signed-in CLI users by their HyperFrames user id, and say signed-in usage is not anonymous (1c3fcf4d2, #6703).
  • Producer: Report which element set the film length (3ca1005a8, #6689).

Fixes

  • Player: iPhone Safari keeps every take's sound after the first seconds of an edit (48ba0e5b2, #6724).
  • AWS Lambda: The release ffmpeg build falls back to verified mirrors when a source host is down (f0665f718, #6728).
  • CLI: A failed render names its failure code for the Desktop app (ba8ffffa0, #6725).
  • CLI: A snapshot still shows what playback shows, whatever other times are asked for (cf1589242, #6720).
  • CLI: The lint summary points at hyperframes lint, not a flag only render takes (98a28cc4b, #6723).
  • Ci: Skills sync steps past the five hidden ClawHub versions blocking it (49e031230, #6713).
  • CLI: An unknown command like search says which command does that (bb4deb801, #6710).
  • CLI: Check says how many browser-playable copies are done while it makes them (2f8c4b1da, #6712).
  • Core: A multi-worker or chunked render lands every frame where an in-order render does (fa9acbd73, #6707).
  • CLI: Check says a lint error is why the browser pass was skipped (11312b359, #6709).
  • CLI: Capture names the network's proxy when it refuses the connection to the site (f0c5db6ea, #6706).
  • CLI: Check waits as long as the --timeout it was given before it counts as idle (6c1765933, #6705).
  • CLI: Audio windows read beats off the whole clip; beats snap to the signal and mark silence (4fa5f5451, #6692).
  • Producer: A BeginFrame capture that draws no first frame in 20 s switches to screenshot (a5496b188, #6701).
  • CLI: Media-treatment --capability preset finds the presets area (cfa653af4, #6700).
  • CLI: A Windows render no longer prints taskkill's already-exited message (ac449555f, #6698).
  • CLI: Snapshot reads https video in the Desktop app instead of failing at ffprobe (0620067eb, #6699).
  • CLI: A stopped model download resumes on the next run instead of starting over (81959201f, #6694).
  • CLI: Media-treatment finds an area by the name its overview prints (2c3d1e07f, #6675).
  • Engine: Downloads refuse hosts that resolve to private addresses (9830059bd, #6571).
  • CLI: Check no longer reports outline text as not painted (9f65efb19, #6693).
  • CLI: Beats --json answers with no track when the composition has no music (ec10aae54, #6690).
  • Core: Graded media honors object-position vertically instead of mirroring it (aa4a71fe4, #6691).
  • Studio: Audio keeps playing after a timeline move or trim in a static composition (9aa6346a7, #6428).
  • Engine: A render from an app that bundles the engine stamps the engine's version (cdc86117c, #6686).
  • Studio: An undo or redo counts as a preview change from its key, not from its reload (710314c15, #6683).
  • Producer: A capture that landed every frame is not failed for the work after its last frame (067cbf7ac, #6674).
  • CLI: Check no longer treats a transparent canvas as covering the frame (203a08dd7, #6681).
  • Core: Grade groups resolve per composition; freeze frames keep their lane (c569760a7, #6685).

Performance

  • Engine: Encode high quality with the x264 fast preset (be05c8693, #6640).
  • Engine: Size render workers from measured heap and the container's CPU quota (cd003e201, #6641).
  • Producer: The audio true-peak check seeks to each segment instead of decoding up to it (c2cde2fb6, #6672).

Docs & Examples

  • CLI: Say which audio probes run once per file (5d437d5ee, #6731).
  • CLI: List check's audio findings with severity, trigger and fix (fa97f9d4b, #6730).

Catalog

  • Skills: The registry skill says its commands are catalog and add (1428f8768, #6680).

Internal

  • One regression shard writes the test image cache (74f323473, #6729).
  • CLI: The publish abort test checks the summary hint is absent (077de9bfe, #6726).

HyperFrames v0.8.213

  • Core: Bundled fonts in a linked stylesheet or a font shorthand are seen by the bundler (67c7ec27d, #6665).
  • CLI: Website capture ends with what it has instead of running until it is killed (5b5633fbb, #6662).
  • Core: A preview media load that fails is reported, not swallowed (cf627a861, #6666).
  • CLI: Snapshot decodes each video once for all the times it shows (cd3b90d1e, #6667).
  • CLI: Check treats text inside a closed details element as not rendered (a1733f4e4, #6669).
  • CLI: Check probes each audio file once, however many clips are cut from it (8fe0ce660, #6654).
  • Studio: A timeline edit on a clip the file lacks says how the clip was looked up (40c28e10d, #6661).
  • CLI: A late teardown error no longer turns a finished command into exit 1 (5a9fdd524, #6659).
  • Engine: Late-starting MP4 and WebM videos render every frame the preview shows (437784038, #6664).
  • Audio: A sound is voice only with a voice-range pitch, judged on the whole clip (1df31d871, #6653).

Internal

  • Producer: Stall test no longer waits a real second for the progress flush (6486d5802, #6658).
  • Engine: The multi-chunk encode test gets the 30 s the other ffmpeg tests have (4db1214e7, #6663).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.212...v0.8.213

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.21.40 Stable2026-10-29T08:02:58Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.40

0.21.40 is a patch release that fixes OMX session-pointer ownership when a long-lived managed Codex app-server is running and improves tmux shim-log test reliability.

Highlights

  • MANAGED CODEX SESSION POINTER OWNERSHIP (#3949; fixes #3946): A verified codex app-server --managed-daemon process is no longer republished as the OMX session owner or allowed to block omx resume and fresh launches. omx session pointer recover and doctor provide a working recovery action. Live interactive OMX owners remain protected; unknown or mismatched process identity fails closed. Reported by @nascyimento.

Fixes and compatibility

  • No breaking API changes.
  • Preserve completed, trailerless non-tail tmux shim-log records when command writes interleave; discard only an unfinished final segment (#3941). This is test-harness coverage, not a runtime behavior change.
  • The release-train commits synchronize the package, npm lockfile, Cargo workspace/lockfile, and plugin metadata at 0.21.40 (#3943).

Known issues and exclusions

  • #3898 remains held for the owner design decision. Related PRs #3939, #3900, and #3907 remain excluded, as does held PR #3915.
  • No other user-facing changes in the compare range are excluded.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title Impact
#3941 @Yeachan-Heo test: keep interleaved tmux shim records Preserve complete interleaved non-tail shim records and verify unfinished-tail safety
#3943 @Yeachan-Heo chore(dev): bump base version to 0.21.40 Synchronize development package and plugin/Cargo version surfaces
#3944 @Yeachan-Heo docs: record v0.21.39 publication evidence Carry prior-release publication evidence onto dev
#3945 @Yeachan-Heo back-merge: main to dev for v0.21.39 Internal merge aligning dev with the prior shipped main
#3949 @Yeachan-Heo fix(session): don't let a managed Codex daemon own the OMX session pointer Recover from a verified managed-daemon owner without stopping it; protect live interactive owners and fail closed on uncertain identity

Validation

  • Pinned dev candidate 87ace943784fa5873e5f8b39edc6686d8bbadde8 passed CI run 40824315726: latest-per-name checks were 18 success, 7 skipped, and 0 failures.
  • Both macOS Ralplan preflights passed: Node 20 (job 123283770572) and Node 26.5.1 (job 123283770409); CI Status passed (job 123288130258).
  • Release-branch local gates and release review/publication evidence are recorded in release readiness.

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

v0.21.39 Stable2026-10-28T17:41:38Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.39

0.21.39 is a patch release that recognizes GPT-6.1 Terra in the known Codex model catalog and fixes canonical state-root publication for detached session leaders.

Highlights

  • GPT-6.1 TERRA MODEL RECOGNITION (#3933): Add gpt-6.1-terra to the known Codex model catalog and routing reference; explicit model strings and existing model defaults remain unchanged.
  • CANONICAL DETACHED-SESSION ROOTS (#3935): Record the canonical state root held by the leader's binding so the strict reuse guard recognizes sessions started through an aliased or symlinked root.

Fixes and compatibility

  • No breaking API changes.
  • Test harness reliability improved: per-file HOME isolation (#3936), VSCode log-write completion synchronization (#3937), and deterministic tmux/team fixture boundaries (#3938).

Known issues and exclusions

  • #3898 (live-pin fix) remains held for the owner design decision. On Codex 0.164.0, .omx-live-pin files in the plugin cache can invalidate hook approval; the workaround posted in #3898 disables the oh-my-codex-local plugin. Related PRs #3900 and #3907 remain open and are not included in this release.

Validation

  • Pinned dev candidate 4c98c4775b0d33f78bdee02bb15ce43aa0505470 passed CI run 40715501337: latest-per-name checks were 18 success, 7 skipped, and 0 failures.
  • macOS Ralplan preflight security passed on Node 20 (job 122912178834) and Node 26.5.1 (job 122912178801); CI Status passed (job 122922743319).
  • Release-branch local gates and review/publication evidence are recorded in docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.39.md.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title Impact

#3935 @ev78394 fix: publish canonical detached-session state roots Store canonical binding root in detached leader state
#3936 @ev78394 test: isolate caller homes for each test file Prevent caller HOME/USERPROFILE from contaminating tests

Contributors

View release →

v1.4.244 Stable2026-10-29T03:55:29Z

Faster diff view and editor, more languages, and better remote files 🌍

This release adds four interface languages, makes switching workspaces faster by preparing the one you’re about to open, and lets files on SSH hosts and paired servers play, save and open like local ones. It also brings smoother chat streaming, a faster prompt in new Windows terminals, steadier WSL terminals, and many fixes for dictation on the phone.

The short version

  • Four new languages: German, Russian, Turkish and Traditional Chinese join the language picker, and many more dialogs, menus and shortcuts are translated. German is machine-translated, and reviews from native speakers are welcome.
  • Faster workspace switching: Hovering a workspace in the sidebar gets it ready, the workspaces next to the current one stay warm, and the switch itself does less work. Dragging a row never switches.
  • Remote files that work like local ones: Play audio and video from SSH hosts and paired servers, save remote text files up to 50 MB, open folders too big for one reply as you scroll, and never open a remote binary as text.
  • Smoother chats: Long replies stream with less stutter, @file suggestions keep up in large repositories, and multi-agent Codex chats no longer grow the local chat history to gigabytes. Claude chats can rewind to before your first message.
  • Steadier terminals: New Windows terminals show their prompt right away instead of after about 3 seconds, closing or resizing a terminal no longer freezes the others on macOS and Linux, and WSL terminals keep their session and folder when their distro stops.
  • Browser: Clear Google cookies from a profile when Google sign-in is stuck on an old account, import cookies from Vivaldi, and Orca no longer quits at startup on Windows and Linux when an extension’s helper window closes.
  • Fixes from the community: Kimi K3 for commit messages, Devin Desktop in Open In, highlighting for Glimmer, CUDA, Groovy and shebang scripts, orca worktree create --branch, ~ in the worktree location, MacPorts tools on macOS, and the faster Windows prompt.

Phone & web updates: Dictation on the phone keeps recording through a reconnect, keeps your words through calls and busy taps, gives your audio back when it stops, and explains setup before asking for the microphone. The mobile setup page now sends iPhone installs to the App Store. Korean 10-Key typing, held terminal keys, pairing and file saves on the phone work better, and the phone does less work in the background.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Same terminal host as v1.4.243: This release keeps v1.4.243’s terminal host instead of starting a fresh one, so terminals open when you update keep running and stay attached. A few terminal fixes run inside the terminal host, such as the faster prompt in new Windows terminals, closing or resizing a terminal without freezing others, and Ctrl+C in Codex. They take effect once the terminal host restarts, for example after you restart your computer.
  • SSH hosts and paired servers: Some remote fixes need the host’s Orca server on this version too: saving large files, opening very large folders, theme answers for Codex in paired terminals, and copying from Codex started by hand (open a new remote shell after the host updates). Older servers keep their current behavior.
  • SSH workspaces: Deleting a workspace on an SSH project now offers Remove from Orca, which leaves its files on the host untouched.
  • Workspace switching: Workspaces still switch on click. Holding the next or previous workspace shortcut (Cmd+Shift+Up/Down) now moves the sidebar highlight and switches once you let go.
  • Worktree location: A location starting with ~ now means your home folder. A ~ value saved by an earlier version is expanded on the next start.
  • Word separators: Double-click always breaks words at spaces and tabs, even if your custom Word Separators setting leaves them out. The saved setting doesn’t change.
  • Mobile setup: The iOS button, QR code and copied link on the mobile setup page point to the App Store. The TestFlight preview option is gone; Android still uses the APK link.

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
studio-v2.55.0Reasonix Studio v2.55.0 Stable2026-10-29T05:30:11Z

本版新增可自定义的对话宽度百分比，调整设置页与安装升级的体验，并修复 Windows 沙箱下 Git Bash 不可用等多处问题。

新增

  • 「外观 → 尺寸 → 行宽」新增自定义百分比，可在 30–100% 之间按 1% 调整，对话与输入框一起生效，不影响已有预设。关联 #14649 fixed in #14667 by @lancy69。

变更

  • 设置页顶部的标题块（分类名、设置项数量）去掉了，导航已标明位置，第一张卡片直接出现在页面顶部。关联 #14625。
  • 已安装的程序位于项目文件夹内时，「用其他程序打开」会列出它并标明原因，悬停可看是哪个文件夹，不再悄悄消失。关联 #14627。
  • 安装升级时 Studio 还在运行，安装程序会连同内核一起关掉，必要时请求一次管理员权限；仍关不掉就用中文说明怎么退出。修复 #14658 fixed in #14659。
  • 设置里的「图形渲染」从「外观与主题」页移到「窗口与性能」页，和其他与占用相关的选项放在一起。修复 #14719 fixed in #14732。
  • 普通目录会话不再显示顶部的「原目录」徽标，独立工作树统一显示「独立工作树」并补充目录说明。关联 #14619 by @SivanCola。

修复

  • Windows 开启沙箱后 Git Bash 每条命令都报「couldn't create signal pipe」；现在 Git Bash 在沙箱下起不来时，会话自动改用 PowerShell 并说明原因。修复 #14713 fixed in #14735，关联 #14714。
  • 设置里主按钮（编辑模型后的「保存」、「新建预设」、「加入 QQ 群」）的文字与底色几乎同色、看不清的问题。修复 #14679 fixed in #14733。
  • 输入框下方的执行权限、模式、模型、强度菜单展开时，不再被上方的计划条盖住一截。修复 #14720 fixed in #14731。
  • 对话里「执行过程」一行的字号与同级的「思考」一行统一。修复 #14682 fixed in #14686。
  • 设置按钮高度对齐、重命名对话框字体统一，项目栏的「添加项目」在长列表滚动时保持可点。关联 #14600 fixed in #14708 by @lancy69、#14601 fixed in #14707 by @lancy69、#14607 fixed in #14709 by @lancy69。
  • 恢复会话时，纯图片的一轮不再消失，终端会显示「附有 N 张图片」。关联 #14716 by @chuiwenwei。
  • reasonix doctor catalogs 不再对记录过失败的目录打印 ok，而是显示 failed 或 degraded。关联 #14549 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 会话租约无法证明时不再谎称被占用：密钥擦除拒绝改写并以错误退出，会话与任务列表显示 unknown。关联 #14727 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 以 .. 开头的根目录名现在按仓库内路径判断 gitignore。关联 #14734 by @kevin9327。
  • 后台子任务的用量保留启动时的 Goal 归属，嵌套委派继承原归属；子任务恢复拒绝无法识别的持久化状态。关联 #14025。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@lancy69、@SivanCola、@chuiwenwei、@kevin9327

<!-- lang:en -->

New

  • Appearance → Size → Line width has a custom percentage, 30–100% in 1% steps, applied to the transcript and composer together; the presets are unchanged. Refs #14649 fixed in #14667 by @lancy69.

Changed

  • The title block at the top of each settings page (section name and setting count) is gone; the navigation already shows where you are. Refs #14625.
  • An installed program inside a project folder is listed in "Open with" as unavailable, with the reason and folder on hover, instead of silently missing. Refs #14627.
  • During install or upgrade the installer closes a running Studio with its kernel, asks once for administrator permission if needed, and says how to quit by hand. Fixes #14658 fixed in #14659.
  • "Graphics rendering" moves from the Appearance and theme page to the Window and performance page, next to the other options that affect resource use. Fixes #14719 fixed in #14732.
  • Ordinary sessions no longer show the Local checkout header badge; isolated worktrees use one neutral label with explanatory tooltips. Refs #14619 by @SivanCola.

Fixed

  • With the Windows sandbox on, every Git Bash command failed; a sandboxed session now uses PowerShell and says why when Git Bash cannot start there. Fixes #14713 fixed in #14735, refs #14714.
  • Primary buttons in Settings (Save after editing a model, New preset, Join the QQ group) no longer draw their label in almost the same colour as their fill. Fixes #14679 fixed in #14733.
  • The permission, mode, model and effort menus under the composer are no longer partly covered by the plan bar above them. Fixes #14720 fixed in #14731.
  • The "Execution" row in a conversation uses the same text size as the "Thinking" row at the same level. Fixes #14682 fixed in #14686.
  • Settings button heights line up, the rename dialog uses the shared type styles, and the project header's Add project stays reachable while a long list scrolls. Refs #14600 fixed in #14708 by @lancy69, #14601 fixed in #14707 by @lancy69, #14607 fixed in #14709 by @lancy69.
  • A restored transcript no longer drops a turn that was only an image; the terminal says "N image(s) attached". Refs #14716 by @chuiwenwei.
  • reasonix doctor catalogs no longer prints ok for a catalog that recorded a failure; it reports failed or degraded. Refs #14549 by @chuiwenwei.
  • When a session lease cannot be proven, the secret scrub refuses to rewrite and exits non-zero instead of claiming the lease is held; listings show unknown. Refs #14727 by @chuiwenwei.
  • A root name starting with .. is judged as inside the repository for gitignore. Refs #14734 by @kevin9327.
  • Background children keep the Goal that started them for usage accounting, nested delegation inherits it, and subagent continuation refuses unrecognised persisted states. Refs #14025.

Thanks to this release's contributors: @lancy69, @SivanCola, @chuiwenwei, @kevin9327

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-29T05:19:43Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1963+g9290926ccf
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792527110

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

anomalyco/opencode ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2.2.1 Stable2026-10-29T05:16:55Z

Highlights

  • Keep Working Toward a Goal: Run /goal <objective> in the TUI or Desktop to let the session continue until a reviewer confirms the objective is met. Use /goal pause, /goal resume, or /goal clear to control it. (#59437)
  • Generate Images: Enable experimental.image_generate to let agents create or edit images with the new image_generate tool. You can optionally set its default model with experimental.image_generate.model. (#59939, #60096)
  • Inspect Background Shells: Click a running shell in the Desktop activity pill to see its live output and stop it without leaving the menu. (#59496)
  • New Default Model Picker: Search favorites, recent models, providers, and variants from the redesigned model picker, which is now the default in Desktop. (#59997)

Breaking changes

@opencode/ai provider removals and renames

These changes affect code that imports @opencode/ai directly. They don't change the CLI, Desktop, or the HTTP API.

  • The fal, Black Forest Labs, Replicate, and Stability providers are removed, along with their ./providers/fal, ./providers/black-forest-labs, ./providers/replicate, and ./providers/stability entry points. (#59977)
  • Five legacy entry points are removed. Import the nested paths instead, which 2.2.0 already exports: ./providers/amazon-bedrock-mantle → ./providers/amazon-bedrock/mantle, and ./providers/google-vertex-chat, -interactions, -mistral, and -responses → ./providers/google-vertex/chat, /interactions, /mistral, and /responses. (#59506)
  • Each provider's configure() input type is now named Config, for example Groq.LanguageModelOptions → Groq.Config, and model() settings are optional when every setting is optional. (#59765)

Core

Improvements

  • Add /goal <objective> with pause, resume, and clear controls, plus automatic progress review until the objective is met. (#59437)
  • Add opt-in scheduled prompts through /schedule, agent tools, and experimental.schedules. (#59401, #59457)
  • Add the opt-in image_generate tool for background image creation and editing, with automatic or configured model selection. (#59939, #60096)
  • Offer GPT Image 2 after Codex sign-in and add MiniMax and Meta Muse image generation support. (#60085, #59929, #60081)
  • Add Responses API support for OpenRouter, DigitalOcean, and Venice, including freeform tools. (#59991, #59963)
  • Improve snapshot performance by coalescing captures and avoiding repeated ignore-file work. (#59419)

Bug fixes

  • Preserve more context headroom when fitting tool output into the context window. (#59934)
  • Retry provider throttling correctly instead of reporting it as context overflow. (#59649)
  • Send image-edit references and image sizes in the formats expected by OpenRouter, Google, and Vercel Grok. (#60067, #59927)
  • Keep credential metadata and prompt-cache settings out of model settings and variants. (#60057, #59500)
  • Keep Git snapshots working in repositories that use a split index. (#59417)
  • Expose Console web search only when the workspace enables it. (#59592)
  • Improve Code Mode diagnostics for parse errors, stalled awaits, and promise-like search results. (#59834, #59749, #59981)

TUI

Improvements

  • Show /goal status below the prompt and provide a dialog for pausing, resuming, or clearing it. (#59437)

Bug fixes

  • Show execution failures that occur before the first model step. (#60079)
  • Keep the activity spinner visible while a continued subagent runs. (#59950)
  • Send pasted image and PDF paths correctly when connected to a local server. (#59833)
  • Improve spacing in an empty /usage dialog. Thanks @Tarquinen. (#59918)

Desktop

Improvements

  • Add /reload to reload configuration without restarting. (#59887)
  • Make the redesigned searchable model picker the default, with dedicated Favorites and Recent sections and keyboard-accessible provider controls. (#59997)
  • Show live output and a Stop action when you select a background shell from the activity pill. (#59496)
  • Show /goal state and actions in a composer pill. (#59437)
  • Redesign the integrations dialog and always include OpenCode free models in the model picker. (#59976, #60047)
  • Add project, worktree, open-in-app, and terminal actions to the session location menu. (#59607)
  • Refine project and worktree selection, command search, prompt-history focus, composer model labels, and artifact preview controls. (#59397, #59604, #59626, #59735, #59657)
  • Highlight quoted timeline content while you write its first comment. (#59286)
  • Keep draft attachment bytes local when using experimental media URIs. (#59104)

Bug fixes

  • Follow each plugin server’s configured endpoint when connecting to its event stream. (#60087)
  • Treat the reachable scroll boundary as the end when Desktop zoom is active. (#60034)
  • Prevent shell text from showing through copy buttons and avoid restyling the transcript when closing a side panel. (#59901, #59878)
  • Keep rendering malformed tool metadata instead of crashing the timeline. (#59736)
  • Make dropdowns open immediately and keep the new-session location shortcut working after tooltip interactions. (#59268, #59680)
  • Remove edge halos from macOS application icons. (#59900)

SDK

Improvements

  • Add client.experimental.schedule.{create,list,get,pause,resume,delete} for managing scheduled prompts. (#59457)
  • Group experimental generated-client methods under client.experimental; previous locations remain as deprecated aliases until the next minor release. (#59072)
  • Expose compaction through the Promise AI client. (#59987)
  • Add freeform tool support and Responses routes for OpenRouter, DigitalOcean, and Venice. (#59963, #59991)

Extensions

Improvements

  • Add the experimental experimental.idle Session hook so plugins can continue work before a session goes idle. (#59437)
  • Let Code Mode extension globals return Effects and declare required services. (#59948)
  • Add custom composer-pill popovers for GUI plugins. (#59437)

teng-lin/notebooklm-py ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.8.6 Stable2026-10-29T04:50:05Z

Added

  • Deferred audio generation (#2506). Queue audio overviews with later=True in Python or --later in the CLI on Web and Android.

  • Deferred Studio generation (#2519). Queue videos, reports (including study guides), quizzes, flashcards, infographics and slide decks with later=True in Python or --later in the CLI on Web and Android. Scheduled jobs return scheduled; combining --later with --wait is rejected. Cinematic video remains unsupported for deferred generation.

  • Experimental Android Live Voice (#2509). Android clients can open notebook-bound client.live.connect(...) sessions with audio input/output, transcripts, interruption events and bounded lifecycle cleanup. Recording, playback and codecs remain application-owned; automatic reconnection is not enabled.

Changed

  • The Windows CLI runs on asyncio's default event loop (#2485). The notebooklm CLI no longer installs WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy at startup (#79). On CPython 3.12–3.14 that loop could drop chunks of large file uploads (AssertionError: Data should not be empty, then NETWORK_ERROR … (upload_finalize)), and it had already broken Playwright login (#89, #2011). Commands now use the Proactor loop, like notebooklm-mcp, notebooklm-server and the Python API. If commands hang inside a sandbox such as Sandboxie-Plus (#75), set NOTEBOOKLM_WINDOWS_EVENT_LOOP=selector; an unknown value fails before the command starts. On Python 3.11+ the CLI no longer uses the event-loop policy API that Python 3.16 removes, login --master-token now runs through the same loop selection, and the CLI hides curl_cffi's Proactor warning, whose advice to install the selector policy no longer applies.

Fixed

  • Android owned notebooks can be removed from recent views without an error. Google now returns INVALID_ARGUMENT for this no-op. The client verifies explicit ownership before accepting that response; failures for shared, missing, or unreadable notebooks still raise an error.

  • Fresh installs include typing_extensions (#2500). Declare the runtime dependency used by public type exports.

  • Synchronous cookie recovery honors the selected transport (#2504). Curl-backed recovery uses the supported curl_cffi runtime.

  • Updated locked dependencies to address reported vulnerabilities (#2513).

  • An uppercase full UUID now reaches the right notebook, source, note or artifact. The CLI and MCP resolvers passed a full UUID through in the case it was typed, but Google matches ids case-sensitively and its ids are always lowercase. Reads by an uppercase id failed with a misleading NOT_FOUND, and notebook_delete by one reported deleted while the notebook stayed. The resolvers now lowercase a full UUID before any backend call. The REST server, which takes ids as given in the path, is unchanged.

  • Windows CLI uploads on the selector loop no longer lose data (#2485). On Python 3.12–3.14, asyncio's selector loop drops a chunk of the request body when a Windows socket send would block, so multi-megabyte uploads failed with a flood of AssertionError: Data should not be empty and left the source stuck in preparing. When NOTEBOOKLM_WINDOWS_EVENT_LOOP=selector selects that loop, the CLI now installs the fix from Python 3.15, only on interpreters that have the bug. The default Proactor loop, the Python API, notebooklm-mcp and notebooklm-server leave asyncio unpatched.

  • Android audio and video downloads no longer crawl at playback speed. The media host, googlevideo.com, throttles a whole-file download to about 32 KiB/s, so a 23-minute audio overview took roughly 12 minutes and often failed with Android transfer failed (code=transport …). The Android backend now fetches that host in contiguous 8 MiB ranges, which arrive at full speed: the same 22.8 MB file downloads in about a second. Each range is checked against the previous one and the declared total, and a server that ignores the range is still read in full.

  • Title lookups now find İstanbul from istanbul (#2476). MCP name resolution (notebooks, sources, notes, artifacts and Studio items) matches in tiers. Tier 1 is Unicode canonical caseless matching, so composed and decomposed spellings now agree. Only when tier 1 finds nothing does an accent-insensitive tier 2 run, and it raises an ambiguity error rather than pick between several titles. The near-miss hints and the download --name filter use the accent-insensitive key directly. Studio item refs gain the title-prefix matching the other resolvers already had for lookups and previews; studio_rename and a confirmed single-item studio_delete still need an exact title or an id, and a complete Studio listing for a title. Dotless ı still never matches i. The confirmation previews of share_set_access, share_set_user and share_remove_user now include the resolved notebook title, as notebook_delete already did, so a tier-2 match is visible before anything changes; if the notebook list cannot be read, the preview shows title: null instead of failing. These previews, and those of notebook_delete and a single-source source_delete, reuse the list read to resolve a name instead of listing again; the two delete previews also show title: null when a full-id preview cannot read the list (with a name, resolution fails first). Preview titles are now also found when the ref is a full id in uppercase. Tools that change data (renames, saves, adds, generation, research, chat settings, sharing changes, and confirmed deletes) use tier 1 only: a ref that only the accent-insensitive tier would match returns a VALIDATION error naming the candidates, where it was NOT_FOUND with a "Did you mean" hint before. Read-only tools and confirmation previews keep both tiers.

View release →

vercel-labs/agent-browser ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.41.0 Stable2026-10-29T02:52:55Z

Breaking Changes

  • @agent-browser/eve tools now come from the MCP tool catalog. The extension exposes the CLI's MCP tools instead of 21 hand-written ones, so tool names and inputs change: navigate is open, evaluate is eval, press_key is press, select_option is select, set_checked is check and uncheck, get is get_text, get_url and the other get_* tools, tabs is the tab_* tools, and wait_for is the wait_* tools. The same names apply to @agent-browser/eve/tools overrides. The sandbox needs agent-browser 0.41, which the extension installs by default (#2241)

New Features

  • Added agent-browser mcp call <tool> '<json>' to run one MCP tool without a stdio session and print its result as JSON with the agent-browser version, for runtimes that cannot keep a process open between tool calls (#2238)
  • Added @agent-browser/sandbox/mcp-tools.json, every MCP tool with its title, description, input schema, annotations and profiles, generated from the CLI and checked by a test (#2240)

Improvements

  • @agent-browser/eve runs each tool as its own short-lived process, so no agent-browser process outlives a tool call. The 28 core tools are listed to the model and the other 14 are deferred, found with eve__search, which keeps the listed tool context at its previous size (#2241)

Bug Fixes

  • MCP tool arguments can no longer set global flags. The CLI reads global flags anywhere in its arguments, so a value such as a selector or extraArgs entry could carry its own --allowed-domains and override the server or caller allowlist. Tool calls that try are now rejected (#2238)

Contributors

  • @Railly
  • @lahfir

Features

  • My Work / My Focus: Aceternity components across My Work and My Focus (#5136)
  • Observer: opt-in OpenAI-compatible batching (#5117)
  • Work view: summary provider selection is now explicit (#5104)
  • Telemetry: count My Work viewer opens and page switches in PostHog (#5107)

Fixes

Work view / My Work

  • Keep the build story under its cap with part budgets (#5128)
  • Stop failing refreshes from flooding agents and retrying forever (#5108)
  • Preserve rejected answers in refresh corrections (#5113)
  • Render recorded group titles and review summaries (#5114)

Redaction

  • Redact values under bare token, secret and api_key keys (#5130)
  • Redact the bearer token of an Authorization header object (#5129)
  • Pair MYSQL_PWD and dbPassword keys in structured payloads (#5111)
  • Redact Slack app-level (xapp-) and refresh (xoxe-) tokens (#5119)

Antigravity / MCP

  • Record agy MCP calls under their real tool name and a stable id (#5170)
  • Resolve Antigravity project context (#5105)
  • Use %I, not %~I, in the Windows 8.3 short-path lookup (#5099)

Worker, install and agents

  • Guard POST /api/logs/clear against foreign localhost origins (#5123)
  • Port reclaim reads the port owner, command line and name from /proc on Linux (#5101)
  • Clear last-install-error.json when an install or repair finishes (#5102)
  • backup-setup stops when the installed claude-mem has no backups (#5103)
  • Launch Codex through its resolved executable (#5112)
  • Pi: give the session-start context request the hooks' API budget (#5118)

Tests

  • Syntax-check the committed plugin bundles, not the built ones (#5137)

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.51 Stable2026-10-28T22:42:36Z

5.1.49 and 5.1.50 were not published; their changes ship here.

Session startup and task RPC attachment no longer wait for task-host liveness probes. Recovery runs independently per host, stops claiming or reviving children after shutdown, and preserves newer ownership claims. (#11335) DAG recovery and workpool reconciliation now wait for that same session-start reconcile (one shared gate) without holding admission, so a paused DAG's revivable child is never judged orphaned and a queued workpool item of an orphaned worker is redelivered rather than marked delivery_uncertain. (#11525)

Continuing a synchronous task whose session was already deleted now fails at once with an error saying to start a new task, instead of waiting out the 30-minute sync poll timeout; a sync poll also stops when the server keeps reporting its session as not found. Completed sync task sessions now honor background_task.taskCleanupDelayMs instead of a fixed 10 minutes. Reported by @orgito. (#11524, fixes #11520)

omo.dev deploys again after the document-shell change. The /design showcase became a root layout of its own and lost the hourly revalidation it used to inherit, so one prerendered page had no expiry and the deploy's asset-retention check refused the build. The design layout revalidates hourly again, within the site's one-hour HTML lifetime. (#11587, fixes #11585)

Release pull requests no longer fail the changelog gate when fragments for the next release land on the base branch after the cut. (#11574)

  • Release preparation no longer fails its own command-string audit when changelog fragments have been folded into the changelog. (#11562)

The frontend skill now builds in ten habits that design reviewers check for. Before designing a new screen, a redesign, or a design system, it reads the guideline source in full, defines the ideal state per persona and journey step, and records every gap in a ledger. Controls keep the same box size in every state, and a spinner or arrow never adds padding. Pointer targets are at least 24 px, colour roles come before components (built from shadcn/ui anatomy), glass and dated trend surfaces stay out unless the brief asks for them, and the subtract pass counts the controls. Every UI or design-system deliverable states a per-criterion WCAG 2.2 AA result. (#11536, fixes #11534)

macOS computer use releases the keys and buttons it holds even if the engine dies mid-call. An input request that presses now starts the engine binary again as a hidden release helper and streams every press, drag move and release to it; when the engine is killed, crashes or a request unwinds with something still held, the helper posts the releases (within 2 seconds) through the same routes, leaving any key or button the user is physically holding to the user. The helper runs in its own session, ignores the hangup and terminate a group kill sends until its releases are out, and refuses to run unless the engine that spawned it is its parent and the stream carries its per-spawn token. Other platforms are unchanged. (#11535)

A process child whose provider has no usable sign-in now moves on to the next provider instead of failing. When the engine refuses a child's first prompt with provider_credentials_missing or provider_credentials_expired, the task skips the remaining models on that provider and starts on the next provider in its fallback chain, ending on the parent session's model when nothing else is left. Each hop is recorded as a task_model_fallback event naming the provider. An engine that does not send these codes behaves exactly as before. Every other refused first prompt in process mode now also carries the engine's validated error code as cause_code in its task_start_failed event and in the start-failure message (for example host_refused: host_memory_pressure); this is diagnostic only and does not change what the task does. (#11440, part of #10806) (#11525)

A queued steer to a subagent keeps its own id across the child boundary, and a lost acknowledgment is checked before anything is sent again. A message queued for a child that is still starting reaches it under the id it was queued with, so a drain repeated after a restart replays instead of delivering a second copy. When the child's acknowledgment is lost, omo asks the child whether it holds that id (get_pending_inputs): held settles it, not held resends the same id, and an unreadable answer keeps it uncertain instead of guessing. (#11427, part of sisyphuslabs/omo-desktop-app#2838) (#11525)

Model-profile notices no longer reach the model. A notice such as "model profile ... has no available model" was sent as part of the conversation, so the model read it ahead of your first message, and on a slow start it could cost an extra model request. The notice now stays out of the conversation: when there is something to do, the desktop app shows it as a notice with the next step (sign in from Provider authentication settings, or choose a model in the composer), and a headless run logs it. (#11365) A stored login that failed while a working provider was used instead still reaches you as a notice naming the provider to re-authenticate, and a profile model the registry no longer finds is reported with the step to choose another model. (#11525)

Memory read grants now check the file a raw-string tool actually opens. Containment follows native filesystem traversal before normalizing paths, so a directory symlink followed by .. cannot redirect a project-memory grant to another identity. Built-in tools retain their resolved-path behavior, missing notes remain readable through their existing canonical ancestor, and case aliases of the same directory remain accepted. (#11339) (#11525)

Completed Momus review verdicts are now persisted as numbered Markdown artifacts next to the plan file. Each .omo/plans/{plan}.reviews/NNN-okay.md or NNN-reject.md artifact captures the verdict, timestamp, model, plan sha256, review duration, reviewer session id, and the verdict block (the reasoning that precedes the verdict is not stored), so a finished review survives the end of the session. A .gitignore written next to the plans keeps the .reviews directories out of the user's own staging area. Momus itself stays read-only; persistence happens entirely on the orchestrator side after the review result returns. (#11237, fixes #3002) Thanks to @surlymochan for this contribution. (#11525)

Release binaries may be up to 200 MiB, and 5.1.49 was not published. omo 5.1.49's Linux binaries came out over the 182 MiB size budget (linux-x64 200.0 MB, linux-arm64 198.2 MB), so that release stopped before anything was published. The growth is the Bun 1.4.4 runtime itself: an empty compiled Bun program grew from 90.8 MB to 109.1 MB between Bun 1.4.3 and 1.4.4, while everything omo added since 5.1.48 is about 0.46 MB. The budget moves from 182 MiB to 200 MiB so releases keep shipping the Bun version CI tests. (#11491) (#11525)

Every omo.dev page now declares its own language and the dark colour scheme, and starts with a skip link. Korean, Japanese, Chinese and the other locales set <html lang> to their language (it was always English), form controls and scrollbars render dark, the first Tab offers "Skip to content", focused controls no longer hide under the sticky header, and unknown pages show a 404 in the reader's language. (#11498)

The omo.dev docs search box has a visible edge and a real label. Its border now meets the 3:1 contrast floor (it was 1.2:1), screen readers announce it as "Filter documentation sections" instead of its placeholder, and the section list has its own name. (#11492)

Icon buttons on omo.dev show their label on keyboard focus, and Escape closes the mobile menu. The workflow graph's Fit and Center buttons get a real tooltip (shown on hover and focus, dismissed with Escape), and Escape closes the mobile navigation sheet and puts focus back on the menu button. (#11490)

Dimmed graph nodes and quiet labels on omo.dev are readable again. Nodes off the highlighted path in the workflow graph recede by colour instead of fading to 20% (text contrast goes from 1.1:1 to 5.8:1), and step numbers and the corrected word in the demo no longer use the decoration-only grey. (#11488)

The opencode edition's skill tool no longer repeats skill descriptions OpenCode already lists. A skill is left out of the skill tool's item list only when OpenCode core's own skill list has the same skill (same name, same file) with a description, so its description is sent once per request instead of twice. Skills core does not list (flat .md skills, skills without a name or description, skills core skips because of its disable flags or a custom Claude config dir, OMO-bundled skills) stay in the skill tool's list, and when core's list cannot be read nothing is left out. (#11456)

The provider sign-in page shows the OmO logo. Every omo launch path (npm, the compiled binary, the local launcher) hands the engine the OmO icon with its brand, so the page a browser shows after signing in to a provider can carry the OmO mark instead of only the name. The logo appears only once omo pins a senpi release that includes senpi#4432/#4434; the currently pinned senpi (2026.10.27-1) ignores it. (#11445, fixes #11444)

Release preparation no longer fails its own tests. The changelog stamp-agreement test read changes/unreleased unconditionally; a release-state tree has consumed every fragment and has no such directory, so the test crashed on every release PR. It now reads the real fragments only when the directory exists. (#11443)

LazyCodex bug-fix skills now send fixes to the repository that owns the source. lcx-contribute-bug-fix told agents that a LazyCodex-owned defect must become a verified-fix issue carrying the patch on the code-yeongyu/lazycodex mirror, so a fix that already sat as an open lead PR here was filed a second time as a full diff on the mirror. The skill now searches the author's own open PRs across repos first, then opens a fork PR against code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent (base dev, packages/omo-codex/plugin/... paths, changelog fragment); the mirror gets no PR and at most a short issue that links it. lcx-report-bug routes fixes the same way. Upstream openai/codex routing is unchanged. (#11410)

Research fact locators now bind to original snapshot bytes. Source snapshots retain the fetched bytes as raw.bin; a --text file alone becomes a converted-only source that supports warned quotations but no measured rows, and --raw <bytes> --text <extraction> keeps the original next to a declared extraction. Page locators read only reader-visible text nodes (never attributes, scripts, styles, or hidden elements), record byte offsets in the source's own encoding, and fact verify fails closed when a cited snapshot cannot be read. The new source refresh command repins a re-fetched source and re-anchors its rows by quote and context; a legacy row on a refreshed source is listed as not re-anchored and blocks verification instead of passing unchecked. (#11287)

Research collection now catches numbers that text conversion changed, and refuses to register them. source probe-conversion compares numeric multisets with one grouped-number grammar (a footnote digit glued onto 12,345 is reported; a lone 1.234 is a decimal), never groups digits across a table cell, row, list item or line break, lets digits copied from link targets, image sources, alt text and titles cover only numbers inside Markdown link or image syntax, treats a converter's ordered-list markers (only where the source has that <ol> item, lazy 1. 1. 1. numbering included), numeric reference-link labels, footnote-definition labels and hard-wrapped digit groups as conversion syntax rather than numbers, reads a sign through the emphasis, code, titled-link, image and escape markup a converter adds (**2019**-2023 is a range), decodes named references with one table on both sides (&minus;, &thinsp;), reads the raw inline HTML a converter keeps (10<sup>-5</sup>, a sized <img>) as the source's own tags, accepts a reference title on the next line, and counts a minus touching Korean text, a single letter or an exponent, or written as an en dash. source snapshot prints the altered numbers, and fact add refuses a value its source's conversion altered with conversion_altered_number; the recovery is source snapshot --id S<n> --text <verified.txt> --replace, refused while an active row cites the source. The fact-ledger reference declares the probe's guaranteed scope and lists what is out of scope (shortcodes, LaTeX, non-CommonMark tables), which always reports as altered. Both research editions describe this flow; their existing phase instructions now live in focused references. (#11286)

Skill MCP clients can use MCP 2026-07-28 servers. Modern servers receive discovery and per-request protocol metadata without an initialization handshake. Input-required operations stop with a clear error because skill MCP does not support elicitation. Legacy servers receive a discovery probe before their existing initialization negotiation; a timed-out probe logs the fallback and is paid only once per server per process, because later connects to that server go straight to legacy initialization (a failed legacy initialization forgets it), and unsupported-version fallback preserves the legacy server's capabilities and identity. (#11243, part of #11205)

The desktop-engine MCP server also serves MCP 2026-07-28 clients. Discovery and per-request protocol metadata work without initialization; modern results identify the server and complete result type, and tool lists include cache hints. Unsupported request versions are rejected before daemon access, notifications receive no reply, and legacy initialization keeps its existing supported-version negotiation. (#11239, part of #11205)

Fixed

  • Atlas schedules its delayed boulder-continuation retry through an injectable scheduler (default: the real timer), and its tests drive that scheduler instead of patching global timers, so a whole-suite run no longer fires other modules' timers in the retry tests (#11619, fixes #11617)
  • Cancelling a prompt now stops an in-flight skill MCP call: skill_mcp passes the session abort signal to the MCP request, which rejects at once with the abort reason, and an HTTP MCP server's request and SSE stream are closed instead of staying open until the connection is torn down (#11592)
  • The Prometheus planning-file guard resolves Windows paths the way the executor opens them: both / and \ split the physical walk, so a link/.. spelling is followed through the link instead of being collapsed lexically (a symlink escape was allowed), a symlinked or junctioned planning directory such as .omo -> docs\plans is recognised again, and 8.3 short names, drive-letter case and directory case all resolve to one canonical path. Inside-planning checks compare the canonical paths exactly, which keeps case-variant entries on case-sensitive directories distinct (#11548)
  • A ChatGPT subscription whose sign-in refresh fails only briefly at session start (connection refused, timeout, a 408/429/5xx from the token endpoint) now stays selected in the model profile. Before, the session moved to the next provider, for example a configured OpenAI API key, and stayed on that metered lane until it was restarted. A refused refresh still skips the provider with the re-authenticate notice, a credential failure seen while re-checking the model still skips the provider with the matching notice, a model whose own request headers fail to resolve is still skipped, and a candidate kept while its refresh is still unresolved logs one warning with the error class and transport code so a later first-turn failure can be traced (#11442, fixes #11432) (#11525)
  • omo logout now leaves a small sign-out note (no credentials, just the device and the time), so OmO Desktop can tell a deliberate sign-out from a credential store that hasn't answered yet. omo account token and export report it with exit code 10; exit 4 still means nothing could be read. Signing in again removes the note. The note is written before the credentials are removed, so a failed write leaves you signed in and omo logout reports the error; a damaged note is ignored (exit 4) and never breaks omo account show. (#11441, fixes #11434) (#11525)
  • A workflow (DAG) category node whose whole model chain is spent or signed out now finishes on the parent session's model, the same last rung a task child gets. The model is captured when the workflow starts and kept on the run, so retried and recovered nodes keep it too. (#11439) (#11525)
  • Prometheus checks and executes the same authorized planning-file path, including relative symlink/.. paths, aliased project roots, hashline renames, and patch moves. Patch headers must round-trip through the supported executor parser without changing authorized operations or targets. Rename/delete preserve named final links; content writes follow only allowed targets. The physical planning directory may have any name the user chose (for example .omo -> docs/plans) but must be strictly inside the real project root, never the root itself or an ancestor; a .omo path segment matches with filesystem-native casing. Every denial is logged with its specific reason. Nested plans and parent-folder Windows paths remain supported; required argument rewrites fail closed if the host's retained arguments cannot receive them (#11424)
  • Bundled skills no longer ship a top-level __tests__ or *_test folder. Only a skill's top-level test/ and tests/ folders are exempt from the test-folder filter; before, any top-level folder name escaped it. (#11398, fixes #11393)
  • Prometheus now checks every apply_patch Add, Update, Delete, and Move target using the executor's line-splitting and header-slicing rules, including carriage returns and Unicode line separators; empty or unclassifiable headers fail closed (#11392)
  • Prometheus allows plan updates beside Markdown horizontal rules and triple-emphasized text by treating Update context as content, while still checking subsequent mutation headers (#11392)
  • omo run starts its OpenCode server with a per-run password when OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD is not set, so a web page on another localhost port can no longer drive it; attaching to that server from outside the run now needs the password (#11370)
  • Authenticated tmux panes receive credentials through owner-only, one-use files instead of process arguments; run cleanup removes any unread files (#11370).
  • Research fidelity verification now blocks only the facts whose source snapshot is unreadable, names the cause, accepts grouped derived values such as "3,234 KRW-e8" exactly as fill writes them, and detects edits to the original value or unit shown beside a conversion, in any bracket style (ASCII, fullwidth, square, lenticular, corner or tortoise-shell), a missing or misplaced original, a non-numeric bracket, a dash in place of brackets, and a second original (a bare figure in the operand's dimension right after the conversion), in HTML and Markdown editions; another filled fact, a parenthetical figure in prose, a year, or a range after a conversion no longer blocks an untampered report. (#11310)
  • Verified and assumed claim placeholders now fill with their claim marks in HTML and Markdown verification; format guidance reflects Markdown support. (#11310)
  • Research fidelity verification binds the unit written right after a value-only fact, with or without a space and with a Korean particle, a currency sign before it, its unit placeholder, or its table column header by visual column, so hand-edited units block and ordinary prose such as "m in total length" no longer raises a false unit mismatch. A scale word inside the value ("3,234억", "12 million") is part of the value, and a row header in a key/value table is never read as a column header. A qualifier after the value ("초과", "이상", "or more") or an ordinary word that is also a unit alias ("won awards", "second place") is never read as a unit; a unit wrapped in inline markup, in parentheses or after a line break is read; and fill records each value's bound unit, mode included, in the fill map, so an edit to an unknown unit, a deleted unit, or a deleted data-fact-mode attribute still blocks. The longest unit written after a value is the one checked, so a longer unit that starts with the row's unit ("12% 포인트", "12 percent points" for a row in %) blocks, and an ordinary unit word beside a currency sign of another currency ("$320 pounds") blocks. (#11301)
  • First-person verification cues ("we verified", "we double-checked", and the Korean equivalents) warn only when the clause names a result and no document before the clause ends ("We measured a latency of 40 ms, as this report shows." and "We verified the value on page 3." warn); one cue list covers English and Korean. (#11301)
  • Research fidelity verification now matches complete source revisions (a sentence-final period included), reports a missing operand timestamp and a missing operand row under their own codes instead of a window mismatch, and reads quoted CSV labels correctly. (#11290)
  • Polished report editions retain their original brief binding in a separate <out>.polished.fill-map.json, the pre-polish fill map is never rewritten (so fact pairs --changed-since still re-checks every sentence that changed, including after polishing in place), a fill map that does not match the report is refused, and editions without a fill map cannot bypass a changed brief. (#11290)
  • Fact derivation requires caller-supplied observation time (fact derive --now; without it the command refuses with missing_now) and preserves ledger validation errors. Quote exact rules, negative zero, source-value rendering, placeholder identifiers, and literal-brace guidance are corrected. (#11290)

Added

  • CI now fails a pull request that adds an unused file, an unused export, an unused dependency, or an unlisted or unresolved import (bun run knip:check, knip 6.43.0). Findings already on dev are scoped ignores in knip.jsonc, each with its reason. (#11481, fixes #11433)
  • Add the OpenSSL hash-directory selection for the planned engine CA importer: it reads only the hash files Bun's own lookup can reach, walking constructed <hash>.N names and stopping at the first failure, so an orphan, misnamed or post-gap file is never trusted. Nothing uses it yet. (#11430, part of #10762)
  • Add the OpenSSL default-file rule for the planned engine CA importer: a default file Bun would reject, or one holding a certificate it indexes but cannot parse, now contributes nothing as a whole, so the importer never trusts a root that Bun itself cannot reach. Nothing uses it yet. (#11411, part of #10762)
  • Add the Linux trust-bundle selector for the planned engine CA importer: it picks the one distribution-generated TLS bundle from os-release (Debian, Fedora and SUSE families, with no cross-family fallback) and reads it with an 8 MiB cap and a replacement check. Nothing uses it yet. (#11407, part of #10762)
  • Add the v1 engine trust policy, the pure decision behind the planned engine CA importer: from per-OS certificate facts it selects the SSL-server roots and chain-building intermediates, and it applies every OS Deny or Disallowed entry. Nothing uses it yet; the engine still trusts the OS store through NODE_USE_SYSTEM_CA. (#11405, part of #10762)

Security

  • Capture the host execution arguments before the complete before-hook chain, rejecting detached or subsequently changed Prometheus approvals. Tests also cover aliased temporary roots rather than assuming temporary paths are canonical. (#11424)
  • Known and not fixed here: a hard link already under .omo can share an inode with a file outside .omo; realpath does not provide inode isolation. (#11424)
  • Known and not fixed here: TOCTOU between tool.execute.before and the tool's open. Closing this race requires tool-side O_NOFOLLOW and directory-handle confinement. (#11424)
  • Known and not fixed here: the patch round-trip trusts the vendored parser pinned to OpenCode 32f278b4; if the host parser changes, checked and executed targets can diverge again. (#11424)

Also in this release: the changes prepared for 5.1.49

Error messages name a step that works today. While terminal sign-in is closed, every "run omo login" hint (sign-in, omo sync, expired or unreadable sign-ins, memory sync) points at the OmO desktop app instead, and keeps naming omo login once accounts open. A refused service request says whether to sign in again, wait, try later or update omo, instead of always blaming sign-in. A missing plugin file, omo thread and omo update on a broken current install print the exact reinstall command, and /computer says why computer use is unavailable. (#11390, part of #11379) (#11417)

Maintainers can inspect or recreate a stuck get.omo.dev channel pointer from the installer mirror workflow. A manual run with op=head prints the signed HEAD and GET of channels/latest or channels/beta (status, every response header, body; credentials redacted), and op=recreate deletes the pointer and writes the given version as a new object, then reads it back; it refuses a version that is not fully mirrored or does not fit the channel (a prerelease on beta, a stable release on latest). Both share one concurrency group per channel with a mirror that moves that channel. (#11349, part of #11313) (#11417)

A permission denial or a refusal by the user is final for what the refused call was meant to do. Every model route of Sisyphus, Sisyphus-Junior, Hephaestus and Atlas now ends its prompt (before any prompt_append) with one rule: never reach a refused result with another tool, command, path or host; ask the user. Every ultrawork variant (default, Gemini, GLM, GPT, Codex, planner) and the todo and boulder continuations carry the same rule. A test enumerates every prompt route, so a new route without the rule fails. The continuations say "Proceed without asking whether to continue" instead of "Proceed without asking for permission". Computer-use telemetry still counts a refusal as a permission denial when senpi adds guidance after its first sentence. (#11371, part of code-yeongyu/senpi#4309) (#11391)

Bug reports reach the OmO team again. Every report sent from /bug, /feedback or the desktop's in-app report was refused by the report service, which the app showed as a connection problem. Reports now carry the exact-body SHA-256 and length and a semantic redactor version, as the service requires, and terminal reports use your omo login account when you are signed in; signed-out reports still work, and an expired or unusable sign-in sends the report without your account instead of blocking it. In the terminal, each refusal the service can answer is now named: sign-in refusals point to omo login, a request the service rejects shows its error code and request ID, a service-side failure says to try again later, and only a send with no answer is reported as a connection problem (#11194, #11213). (#11391)

Runs on senpi 2026.10.27-1, which tightens shell permission checks. (#11364)

Memory patches that move a file twice in one change no longer leave the memory repository blocked. A chained move such as a.md -> c.md -> d.md listed the intermediate c.md as a path to commit even though it never existed, so git refused the commit and the next memory write found a dirty tree. The intermediate is now dropped when it never existed and kept when it was a real tracked file. Thanks to @MoerAI for the fix. (#11305, car #11307, part of #10858) (#11363)

Memory notice warnings go through the memory logger, and a failed watermark rename keeps its own error. Directory-sync warnings after a soul or agreement watermark is published are now reported through the memory component's logger instead of a direct console.warn, so the host decides where they go, and a temp-file cleanup failure no longer replaces the original rename error. (#11284, part of #10707) (#11363)

omo.dev/download now redirects temporarily (307) to the public desktop beta page. Browsers no longer cache the redirect, so they will reach omo.dev's own download page once that page is enabled; a browser that already cached the earlier permanent (301) redirect keeps it until that cache entry expires. (#11272, follow-up to #11270) (#11363)

Claude Fable 5.5 leads the Fable chains once released. Once 5.5 is released, every builtin chain or profile that runs Claude Fable 5.1 tries 5.5 first at the same effort, with 5.1 kept as the next step. Until then no builtin default, chain or profile lists 5.5, so 5.1 keeps running (#10798, #10800). (#11350)

Claude Fable 5.5 is recognised ahead of its launch. Model detection, context limits, telemetry and the refusal fallback notice know Claude Fable 5.5. Until it is released, no routing path picks the provisional id without proof the model exists, and catalog data always wins over the launch placeholder (#10798, #10800). (#11350)

The frontend skill's llms.txt fetch allowance covers same-origin pages only, and robots.txt still wins over it. Agents read the pages a site's own llms.txt links on that site, never off-site links, and a robots.txt disallow beats an llms.txt link. (#11338, fixes #11337)

The omo.dev desktop beta button reads naturally in Spanish, Portuguese, French and Chinese. (#11331)

The frontend skill's fetch rule now covers pages a site's own llms.txt links. footer.design's /styles pages were listed as an agent surface while the same file forbade reading HTML pages; agents may now read the pages a site's llms.txt links and still never crawl other HTML pages. (#11323, fixes #11320)

omo.dev's hero links the OmO desktop public beta. A "Get the desktop beta" button next to Get started opens https://get.omo.dev/desktop/beta/ in every site language. (#11315)

The frontend skill routes more design sources and asks for research before a landing redesign. MicroKit, Motion Primitives, Uiverse and 21st.dev join the component catalog with licence, agent surface and measured reduced-motion coverage; section galleries (navbar, pricing, CTA, hero, footer, landing.love, Awwwards) are listed as structure-only references; Mobbin is used only through the user's own connected MCP. A marketing-page redesign writes a four-part research report before the first edit, the draft guard names invented proof, prop decoration and repeated headline formulas, and fix passes after the first build stay scoped to one region. (#11314, fixes #11312)

Bun type declarations now match the Bun 1.4.4 toolchain. Every package manifest pins bun-types or @types/bun to 1.4.4, with the root, web, worker, Codex plugin and both LSP package lockfiles synchronized. The committed Codex installer was regenerated in the pinned Linux container. (#11311)

A session command OmO adds later is no longer sent to an attach-enforcing senpi host without an attachment. The thread client fenced only a fixed list of mutations, while the host refuses every session command from an unattached connection except its read allowlist; under SENPI_RPC_REQUIRE_ATTACH=1 any command missing from the client's list would be refused. The client now fences every session command except the reads the host exempts. (#11278, part of #10944) (#11297)

A summary queued by a session that named only its profile is judged with the account's home again. Since #11202 a queued session summary is judged by the config it was recorded under; when that config named a profile but no home, the consent read had no home at all and fell back to the working directory. It now reads the account's home, as the send gate does. The reference doc also says that summaries queued before #11202 keep the old reading until they expire. (#11275) (#11297)

Memory sync and the memory tool no longer hang while a macOS keychain approval dialog is open. A session's sign-in read now gives up after 5 s with an error that names the "bun wants to access key omo-cli" dialog to answer, instead of waiting with no end. Memory sync skips that pass, logs why, and retries after 15 s, doubling to a 5-minute cap, and the memory tool returns at once. A one-time "Allow" clicked after that 5 s deadline is not kept, so the next sync retry asks again; choose "Always Allow" to stop the prompts. All reads in one process share the one keychain request that is still waiting, so parallel sessions no longer stack a blocked read (and another dialog) per request, and after a late approval the next read goes back to the OS store, so another process's rotated token cannot be replaced by an old answer. omo login, omo whoami, omo account and omo sync still wait for the dialog, and now say so after 2 s. Reported by @floweredao. (#11264, part of #11262) (#11297)

The research delivery barrier checks one meaning record per delivered edition. fact meaning-check --edition <format> writes fidelity-meaning-defects.<format>.json bound to that edition's bytes, so an html report delivered with a byte-different inline, Markdown, or post edition passes once each edition with different bytes is checked (an edition whose bytes match a checked edition, such as a copy at another path or a PDF of the html edition, shares its record), and a stale or unchecked edition refuses alone. A single-edition session keeps using fidelity-meaning-defects.json unchanged. Existing session fidelity records remain blocking even when a delivered row attaches a different clean record, and two paths to one record (including through a symlink) no longer duplicate its defects. (Closes #11241, Part of #11009) (#11289)

CI, release workflows and the devcontainer run Bun 1.4.4. Every oven-sh/setup-bun step installs Bun 1.4.4 instead of 1.4.3, the Bun install-cache keys move to bun-1.4.4-, the devcontainer installs bun-v1.4.4, and script/agent/setup.sh expects 1.4.4. (#10895)

Added

  • Set OMO_BUNDLED_SKILLS_DIR to an absolute directory to share bundled skills across runtime installations. Absent or empty is unchanged. An invalid OMO_BUNDLED_SKILLS_DIR (relative, missing, not a directory, unreadable) now logs ONE error naming the variable and falls back to the plugin-relative bundled skills; task tools and the bare skill commands stay registered. Conditional, user, project, and memory skill roots keep their existing locations. (#11332) (#11417)
  • An explicit skillsDir option now wins over a valid OMO_BUNDLED_SKILLS_DIR; precedence is explicit option, then a valid override, then the plugin default. (#11332) (#11417)
  • The override resolver lives in the bundled-skills component itself (no separate module), which keeps the built omo.js inside its size budget: the change costs 531 bytes instead of 1,899. (#11332) (#11417)
  • Add generation-fenced computer-use stop-authority admission: a newer stop rejects an in-flight activation, and durable completion cannot reopen input. This is the local safety prerequisite for the protected stop broker; existing launch modes retain their behavior. (#11333) (#11363)

Fixed

  • After quitting and reopening the same stopped session, the person's next message releases the Stop and reopens task admission even if an acknowledged paused child cannot restore. That child's failure remains recorded, its acknowledged cutoff is kept, and a later Resume can retry it without a live hold observer. This does not recover a parked queued admission (held_admission) marked lost: after restart, retryLost has no resume credential to re-admit it, so it stays stranded (omo#10864, item 1). (#11401) (#11417)

  • Bundled skills no longer ship test, tests, __tests__, or *_test folders nested below a skill's top level (such as ultimate-browsing's engine/tests), so a test module can no longer run inside the installed app and write caches there. Top-level test/ and tests/ resources still ship. (#11377, part of #11373) (#11391)

  • Running a bundled skill no longer writes files inside the installed app, so OmO Desktop no longer reports its files as changed and stops its local server after the coding-agent-sessions, ultimate-browsing or frontend skills run their Python scripts. A manual ast-grep install now downloads into a user cache folder instead of the skill folder. Python the agent runs now keeps its compiled bytecode in an OmO cache folder instead of next to the script, so your projects still start fast without __pycache__ folders appearing in them; if you set PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE or PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX yourself, OmO leaves both alone. Thanks to @minerva32 for the report. (#11376, fixes #11373) (#11391)

  • Prometheus can no longer write outside .omo/*.md through apply_patch, multiedit or a hashline edit's rename; a write whose target it cannot read is blocked (#11369)

  • Count a Windows stop-hotkey request once in authority mode instead of advancing its stop generation again when the worker completes it. (#11333) (#11363)

  • Codex installations now contain only regular files and directories, preventing dependency symlinks from disabling every OmO hook during plugin fingerprinting. Native comment-checker resolution remains available without npm executable links. (#11328) (#11363)

  • The direct Codex package now includes its local LSP dependency so installation can materialize it instead of leaving a dangling package link. (#11328) (#11363)

  • Hosted tasks retain their original session through missed state probes, report unresponsive and recovered status to the parent, and resume queued steering without creating another child. An overdue unresponsive session is reported ended only after the live host confirms its absence following close, never from an acknowledgement or closed frame alone. (#11317) (#11350)

  • Child activity resets missed heartbeat probes, so a delayed state reply does not label a still-streaming child unresponsive. (#11317) (#11350)

  • Finished resident children report retryable unresponsiveness, and transient liveness oscillations are coalesced instead of repeatedly waking the parent. (#11317) (#11350)

  • Research outcome initialization records the run clock once, from --started-at, an earlier valid manifest, a supervisor's run-state file, or the timestamped session directory name, and refuses impossible, future, or copied start times. (#11302)

  • Claim prefixes (V, A, D) fill through the claim-kind registry, and Sources sections check every sentence holding a numeral that is not a citation field (a year or date, a page, volume, issue, or edition marker, a number inside a URL, DOI, arXiv id, ISBN, quoted or cited title, or a citation tag), so reference entries stay exempt and claim sentences are checked; a number in an unquoted title is checked. A year range and link text count as citation fields, and a digit run glued to letters that are not a unit, multiplier, or currency (a commit hash, an issue number, GPT-4, Q3) is a name rather than a numeral, while 50M, 10k, 3x, x3, 2.5GB, 12%, 5mn, 40pct, 5yrs and 1.234.567 stay numerals, also when a word is glued in front of the measure. (#11302)

  • Keep quoted style end-tag attributes inside the polish markup boundary, report edits adjacent to unfinished character references precisely, and respect Markdown closing-fence indentation. (#11291)

  • Expose whether every polish edit applied through a JSON boolean while preserving partial-output success and accepted-edit counts. (#11291)

  • Memory supervisor integration tests report an exited supervisor's code, stderr and run artifacts when a child start or durable state never arrives, instead of stalling until a timeout. (#11288)
